| wp-no-copy-no-computer-en | en | pass | judge: The partner sent one English ack naming the action, with no question about where to clone or which computer. It opened a task on hel with the repo clone step, and the clone line is Ducter's own by design. It does not claim the typo is fixed. |
| wp-named-computer-ends-at-once-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner routed the work to a new task on hel (the repo at /root/dev/ducter, not the Mac), using codex, and never asked the user or waited on the quota. The user hears only one short Chinese ack that names the action and makes no time promise. Nothing internal or error text is pasted. |
| wp-other-task-names-owned-pr-zh | zh | pass | judge: The review task's report about #10932's failing e2e is the last event, and no partner reply, new task, question, or task_send to the review task appears. The rubric allows silence, and the task that owns #10932 already receives the report, so this passes. |
| wp-task-wait-ended-ask-zh | zh | pass | judge: The reply says the task is still in progress and reports the last readable CI look (2 of 3 done), without claiming a pass or fail, a finished state, or an opened task. It runs to three short sentences rather than the one or two specified, and the 'I'll tell you when there's a result' line is a mild commitment, but neither changes the substance. |
| wp-long-round-ci-zh | zh | pass | judge: The partner sent one short calm Chinese line that states only what the steps show (two checks passed, pull-mem-e2e still running). It asks nothing, makes no card, doesn't imply the run is stuck or slow, doesn't restart the task, and doesn't claim CI passed. |
| wp-long-round-stuck-en | en | pass | judge: The partner told the task to stop retrying and end the round with a report that GitHub's login on hel needs renewing, which is the one clear next step. The user-facing line is short, in English, names what was seen (GitHub rejecting the hel login since 19:01) and the fix (renew that sign-in), and claims nothing beyond the 401 errors shown. |
| wp-changes-requested-before-wait-zh | zh | pass | judge: The reply is three short true Chinese sentences: it says it can't merge yet, names the outstanding change request and the still-running tide check, and promises no date. It is somewhat vague on whether the task already has the change request, since it frames the handoff as future, but it doesn't claim only an approval is missing and asks nothing of the user. |
| wp-partner-meant-for-other-zh | zh | pass | judge: The message went to 小家 with the vaccine and Saturday-morning details, and no reminder was set or question asked here. It is one short Chinese line using 你. The clause saying 小家 will remind the user is a forward-looking promise, not a claim that 小家 has already done anything, so it is a borderline but acceptable pass. |
| wp-partner-other-owns-task-zh | zh | pass | judge: The reply is in Chinese with 你, is two short lines, and says plainly that the partner can't see the dentist booking here. It points to 小家 as the owner of medical matters and offers to pass the question on. It doesn't guess the booking status and doesn't open a task. |

The reply is short, uses 你, and says how to reach it via the stone beside the name. It runs to two sentences rather than one line, but that is a minor wording issue. |
| wp-partner-pass-back-en | en | **fail** | too long (103 > 100 chars); judge: The message went to Ducter via the partner tool, and nothing was opened or acted on for the release. The reply is one short English line saying it was passed to Ducter, and it does not claim Ducter's answer or ask a question. |

## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试，或者帮你查一下邮件里有没有需要处理的事。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else, and let you know when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요, 저는 Ducter예요. 예를 들어 지금 쓰는 프로젝트 저장소에서 挂掉的 테스트를 찾아 고쳐 달라고 하시면 됩니다.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```

  [partner message] ㅇㅇ, 내일 아침에 봐요. 좋은 밤 보내요.

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「確実に届ける」、UDPは「速さを優先して、届くかは保証しない」という違いです。

TCPは接続を確立してから、データが欠けず順番どおり届くように再送や並べ替えを行います。UDPはそうした確認をせず、送りっぱなしにするので遅延が少なく、動画配信やオンラインゲームなどに向いています。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar o limpiar tu rama local que todavía no ha compartido, así el historial queda lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o la comparten varias personas, porque reescribir commits publicados rompe el trabajo de los demás.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据完整有序地送达；UDP 不建连、不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to keep its history clean before you open a PR or pull in the latest base. Merge when others have already pulled the branch, since rebasing rewrites commits they may depend on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用 `bun test` 后面直接跟文件路径就行，例如：

```bash
bun test tests/user.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段字符的测试文件，比如 `bun test user`。要只跑某个测试用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：`bun test -t "登录失败"`。
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database before the change is applied to the data files. After a crash, the database replays or rolls back the logged changes, so committed work survives and partial work doesn't corrupt anything.
```

不在范围内：

<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海 600 元以下</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt"><span class="k-none">还没查</span></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">车次与票价</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<style>
.k-app{padding:16px;background:var(--d-bg);color:var(--d-ink);font-size:15px}
.k-h{font-size:15px;margin:18px 0 8px;color:var(--d-soft)}
.k-card{background:var(--d-card);border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:12px;padding:4px 12px}
.k-row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--d-line)}
.k-row:last-child{border-bottom:none}
.k-row-sub{font-size:12px;color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-row-value{font-weight:600}
.k-good{color:var(--d-good)}
.k-sum{background:var(--d-card);border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:12px;padding:14px}
.k-sum-label{font-size:13px;color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-sum-value{font-size:28px;font-weight:700;margin:4px 0}
.k-sum-sub{font-size:12px;color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-none{color:var(--d-muted);font-size:20px;font-weight:500}
</style>
<script>
function render(d){
  var list=document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML='';
  var trips=(d&&d.trips)||[];
  var under=0;
  trips.forEach(function(t){
    if(t.price<600) under++;
    var row=document.createElement('div');row.className='k-row';
    var main=document.createElement('div');
    var name=document.createElement('div');name.textContent=t.train+' · '+t.depart;
    var sub=document.createElement('div');sub.className='k-row-sub';sub.textContent=t.seat+'，余 '+t.left+' 张';
    main.appendChild(name);main.appendChild(sub);
    var val=document.createElement('div');val.className='k-row-value'+(t.price<600?' k-good':'');
    val.textContent='¥'+t.price;
    row.appendChild(main);row.appendChild(val);list.appendChild(row);
  });
  if(trips.length){
    document.getElementById('cnt').textContent=under+' 班';
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent='更新于 '+(d.updated||'');
  }
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render(ducter.get('board'));
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"board":{"updated":"11-23 早上","trips":[{"train":"G1","depart":"周五","seat":"二等座","price":553,"left":12},{"train":"G3","depart":"周五","seat":"二等座","price":553,"left":4},{"train":"G7","depart":"周五","seat":"二等座","price":626,"left":null}]}}
  [Ducter passed the user's answer to task fares] [Ducter] The person now keeps this work's rounds in their small app "京沪高铁票价看板" (app a1cb28627).   var runs = ducter.get('runs') || [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  document.getElementById('empty').style.display = runs.length ? 'none' : '';
  runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function(r){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">Night ' + r.night + '</div><div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + r.p99 + ' ms · stalls ' + r.stalls + '</div><div class="k-row-value">' + r.writes + '</div>';
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  if (runs.length) {
    var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
    document.getElementById('w').textContent = last.writes;
    document.getElementById('s').textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls;
  }
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":2,"writes":"181k","p99":"4.0","stalls":0},{"night":3,"writes":"182k","p99":"4.1","stalls":0}]}

const HABITS = ducter.get("habits") || [];

function ymd(d) {
  return d.getFullYear() + "-" + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
}
function addDays(d, n) {
  const x = new Date(d);
  x.setDate(x.getDate() + n);
  return x;
}
function streakFrom(set) {
  let d = new Date();
  if (!set.has(ymd(d))) d = addDays(d, -1);
  let n = 0;
  while (set.has(ymd(d))) { n++; d = addDays(d, -1); }
  return n;
}
function bestFrom(days) {
  const sorted = [...new Set(days)].sort();
  let best = 0, run = 0, prev = null;
  for (const day of sorted) {
    const [y, m, dd] = day.split("-").map(Number);
    const cur = new Date(y, m -  … (set);
    const best = bestFrom(ticks[h.id]);
    const chips = days.map(d => {
      const k = ymd(d);
      const label = d.toLocaleDateString("en-US", { weekday: "short" }).slice(0, 2);
      const on = set.has(k);
      return '<button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="' + on + '" data-h="' + h.id + '" data-d="' + k + '" title="' + k + '">' + label + '</button>';
    }).join("");
    return '<div class="k-card">' +
      '<div class="k-row-main">' + h.name + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">' + h.sub + '</div>' +

  }).join("");

  document.querySelectorAll("#list .k-chip").forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => toggle(btn.dataset.h, btn.dataset.d));
  });
  ducter.summary("Today " + doneToday + " of 3 · " + topN + "-day streak");
}
function load() {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("ticks")).then(v => {
    if (v && typeof v === "object") {
      for (const h of HABITS) ticks[h.id] = Array.isArray(v[h.id]) ? v[h.id] : [];
    }
    render();
  });
}
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>

const CATS = ['交通', '住宿', '餐饮', '其他'];

const today = () => new Date().toISOString(). … ender();
});

  save();

});

$('cats').innerHTML = CATS.map(c =>
  '<button class="k-chip" data-cat="' + c + '" aria-pressed="' + (c === cat) + '">' + c + '</button>'
).join('');

  cat = b.dataset.cat;

});

$('date').value = today();

    date: $('date').value || today()
  });

  $('amt').value = '';
  $('form').open = false;
  render();
});

ducter.onChange(() => load());

    render();
  });
}
load();

    var all = items();
    all.push({ id: Date.now(), amt: Math.round(amt * 100) / 100, cat: cat, note: noteEl.value.trim(), date: date });
    ducter.set('expenses', all);
    amtEl.value = '';
    noteEl.value = '';
    cat = null;
    chips.forEach(function (c) { c.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false'); });
    document.querySelector('.k-add').open = false;
    render();
  });

  document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
    var row = ev.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    var id = Number(row.getAttribute('data-id'));
    var item = items().filter(function (e) { return e.id === id; })[0];
    if (!item) return;
    ducter.ask('Remove ' + fmt(item.amt) + ' (' + (item.note || item.cat) + ')?').then(function (a) {
      if (a && /yes|ok|remove|delete|go|好/i.test(String(a))) {
        ducter.set('expenses', items().filter(function (e) { return e.id !== id; }));
        render();
      }
    });
  });

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
  [also used tools] app
```

    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub">Tap a PR once it lands</div>
  </div>

  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>All landed.</div>
</main>
<script>
var ITEMS = ducter.get("items") || [];
var done = {};

function render() {
  var list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = ITEMS.map(function (it) {
    var on = !!done[it.id];
    return '<button class="k-row" data-id="' + it.id + '" aria-pressed="' + on + '" style="width:100%;text-align:left;background:none;border:0;padding:10px 0;display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;cursor:pointer;color:var(--d-ink)">' +
      '<span style="width:20px;height:20px;border-radius:6px;border:2px solid ' + (on ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-line)') + ';background:' + (on ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'transparent') + ';color:#fff;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex:none;font-size:13px">' + (on ? '✓' : '') + '</span>' +
      '<span class="k-row-main" style="flex:1"><span style="display:block;' + (on ? 'text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)' : '') + '">' + it.title + '</span>' +
      '<span class="k-row-sub" style="display:block;color:var(--d-muted)">' + (on ? 'Landed' : it.sub) + '</span></span>' +
      '</button>';
  }).join("");
  var open = ITEMS.filter(function (it) { return !done[it.id]; }).length;
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = open;
  document.getElementById("empty").hidden = open !== 0;
  if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(open + " open");
}

document.getElementById("list").addEventListener("click", function (e) {
  var btn = e.target.closest("button[data-id]");
  if (!btn) return;
  var id = btn.getAttribute("data-id");
  done[id] = !done[id];
  if (window.ducter && ducter.set) ducter.set("done", done);
  render();
});

if (window.ducter && ducter.get) {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("done")).then(function (v) {
    done = v || {};
    render();
  });
  if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get("done")).then(function (v) { done = v || {}; render(); });
  });
} else {
  render();
}
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"412","title":"wey-gu/ducter #412 · compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem #10287 · retry on 502","sub":"CI is failing"},{"id":"1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592 · flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"418","title":"wey-gu/ducter #418 · apps bar","sub":"Draft"}]}

(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我在 TTL 测试那个任务里补一条覆盖正好到期那一刻的测试，补好了告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话（回复你上一条修复的消息时说的）：「这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻」

要做的：
- 在 TTL 测试里再加一条测试，覆盖条目恰好到期的那一刻（假时钟推进到 TTL 整点时，确认该值的过期判断和实现保持一致，并且前一刻仍然有效）。
- 沿用现在的假时钟，不要改回真实计时。

完成标准：
- 新测试通过，并且连续跑 20 次都通过（和上次一样）。
- 报告里附上测试命令和输出的通过数。

不要做的：
- 不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR。
- 不要改 TTL 的过期语义，只补测试；如果发现实现和预期对不上，先报告，不要擅自改实现。

地点：~/dev/foo 仓库，分支 ducter/foo-ttl，在这个分支上提交。
